│             │ │ merge_async_iterators(*iterators)\n        \n  │
│             │ │ count = 0\n        async for idx, item in      │
│             │ │ merged:\n            count += 1\n        \n    │
│             │ │ elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start\n        │
│             │ │ total_time += elapsed\n    \n    avg_time =    │
│             │ │ total_time / num_runs\n    print(f"  Large     │
│             │ │ multiple iterators: {avg_time*1000:.2f}ms avg  │
│             │ │ ({count} items)")\n    return                  │
│             │ │ avg_time\n\n\nasync def main():\n    """Run    │
│             │ │ all benchmarks."""\n    print("=" * 70)\n      │
│             │ │ print("Performance Benchmark:                  │
│             │ │ merge_async_iterators")\n    print("=" * 70)\n │
│             │ │ print()\n    \n    single_time = await         │
│             │ │ benchmark_single_iterator()\n    multi_time =  │
│             │ │ await benchmark_multiple_iterators()\n         │
│             │ │ print()\n    large_single_time = await         │
│             │ │ benchmark_large_single_iterator()\n            │
│             │ │ large_multi_time = await                       │

│             │ │ print("Summary:")\n    print("=" * 70)\n       │
│             │ │ print(f"Single iterator (100 items):           │
│             │ │ {single_time*1000:.2f}ms")\n                   │
│             │ │ print(f"Multiple iterators (300 items):        │
│             │ │ {multi_time*1000:.2f}ms")\n    print(f"Single  │
│             │ │ iterator (1000 items):                         │
│             │ │ {large_single_time*1000:.2f}ms")\n             │
│             │ │ print(f"Multiple iterators (3000 items):       │
│             │ │ {large_multi_time*1000:.2f}ms")\n    print()\n │
│             │ │ \n    # Calculate speedup\n    if multi_time > │
│             │ │ 0:\n        speedup = multi_time /             │
│             │ │ single_time\n        print(f"Speedup (single   │

│             │ │ 0, item\n    except BaseException:\n           │
│             │ │ await iterator.aclose()\n        raise\n       │
│             │ │ await iterator.aclose()\n    return\n```\n\n## │
│             │ │ Performance Improvements\n\n### Benchmark      │
│             │ │ Results\n- **Single iterator overhead**:       │

│             │ │ Why This Matters\nThe optimization eliminates  │
│             │ │ the overhead of:\n1. `asyncio.wait()`          │
│             │ │ calls\n2. Task creation and management\n3.     │
